AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='## Root cause\n\n`MainActivity.onBackPressed()` only checked two things: whether the nav drawer was open, and whether the current fragment was `HomeFragment`. It had no way to ask the currently displayed fragment whether it wanted to consume the back press, so when the orange file-selector bottom sheet was expanded in a tool fragment (Invert PDF, Remove Pages, Split, Merge, Add Images, Remove Duplicates, PDF-to-Image), back immediately replaced the fragment with `HomeFragment` instead of collapsing the sheet.\n\n## Fix\n\n1. **New interface** `app/src/main/java/swati4star/createpdf/interfaces/OnBackPressedInterface.java` with `boolean checkSheetBehaviour()` — returns `true` when the fragment consumed the back press.\n\n2. **`util/BottomSheetUtils.java`** — added a reusable helper:\n```java\npublic static boolean collapseBottomSheetIfExpanded(BottomSheetBehavior sheetBehavior) {\n    if (sheetBehavior == null) return false;\n    int state = sheetBehavior.getState();\n    if (state == BottomSheetBehavior.STATE_COLLAPSED || state == BottomSheetBehavior.STATE_HIDDEN)\n        return false;\n    sheetBehavior.setState(BottomSheetBehavior.STATE_COLLAPSED);\n    return true;\n}\n```\nIt is null-safe and also handles dragging/settling/expanded states. Collapsing via `setState` triggers the existing `BottomSheetCallback`, so the up-arrow animates back correctly.\n\n3. **`activity/MainActivity.java`** — `onBackPressed()` now gives the current fragment the first chance to handle the event, before falling back to the old behaviour (double-back exit on Home, otherwise go Home).\n\n4. **All 7 fragments that host the orange file-selector sheet** (`InvertPdfFragment`, `RemovePagesFragment`, `RemoveDuplicatePagesFragment`, `SplitFilesFragment`, `MergeFilesFragment`, `AddImagesFragment`, `PdfToImageFragment`) now implement `OnBackPressedInterface`:\n```java\n@Override\npublic boolean checkSheetBehaviour() {\n    return BottomSheetUtils.collapseBottomSheetIfExpanded(sheetBehavior);\n}\n```\n\nBehaviour now: sheet open → back collapses it and stays on the fragment; sheet closed → back returns to Home exactly as before.\n\n## Verification\n\n`./gradlew clean assembleDebug` cannot complete in this sandbox — the Android Gradle Plugin 3.2.1 and its dependencies are not in the local Gradle cache and all outbound network access (dl.google.com, jcenter) is blocked, so dependency resolution fails before any compilation task runs (this failure is pre-existing and unrelated to the change).
